 449 | /* Initialize structure containing state | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 450 |  * (RFC 1321, 3.3: Step 3)               | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 451 |  */                                      | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 452 | void FAST_FUNC md5_begin(md5_ctx_t *ctx) | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 453 | {                                        | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 454 |  ctx->hash[0] = 0x67452301;              | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 455 |  ctx->hash[1] = 0xefcdab89;              | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 456 |  ctx->hash[2] = 0x98badcfe;              | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 457 |  ctx->hash[3] = 0x10325476;              | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 458 |  ctx->total64 = 0;                       | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 459 |  ctx->process_block = md5_process_block6 | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 460 | }                                        | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 461 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 462 | /* Used also for sha1 and sha256 */      | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 463 | void FAST_FUNC md5_hash(md5_ctx_t *ctx,  | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 464 | {                                        | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 465 |  unsigned bufpos = ctx->total64 & 63;    | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 466 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 467 |  ctx->total64 += len;                    | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 468 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 469 |  while (1) {                             | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 470 |   unsigned remaining = 64 - bufpos;      | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 471 |   if (remaining > len)                   | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 472 |    remaining = len;                      | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 473 |   /* Copy data into aligned buffer */    | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 474 |   memcpy(ctx->wbuffer + bufpos, buffer,  | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 475 |   len -= remaining;                      | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 476 |   buffer = (const char *)buffer + remain | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 477 |   bufpos += remaining;                   | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 478 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 479 |   /* Clever way to do "if (bufpos != N)  | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 480 |   bufpos -= 64;                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 481 |   if (bufpos != 0)                       | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 482 |    break;                                | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 483 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 484 |   /* Buffer is filled up, process it */  | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 485 |   ctx->process_block(ctx);               | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 486 |   /*bufpos = 0; - already is */          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 487 |  }                                       | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 488 | }                                        | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 489 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 490 | /* Process the remaining bytes in the bu | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 491 |  * in first 16 bytes following RESBUF.   | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
 492 |  * endian byte order, so that a byte-wis | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

 494 |  */                                      | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

1061 |  /* Operators defined in FIPS 180-2:4.1. | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1062 | #define Ch(x, y, z) ((x & y) ^ (~x & z)) | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

1068 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1069 |  /* Compute the message schedule accordi | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1070 |  for (t = 0; t < 16; ++t)                | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1071 |   W[t] = SWAP_BE32(words[t]);            | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1072 |  for (/*t = 16*/; t < 64; ++t)           | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1073 |   W[t] = R1(W[t - 2]) + W[t - 7] + R0(W[ | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1074 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

1079 |  e = ctx->hash[4];                       | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1080 |  f = ctx->hash[5];                       | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

1082 |  h = ctx->hash[7];                       | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1083 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1084 |  /* The actual computation according to  | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1085 |  for (t = 0; t < 64; ++t) {              | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

1940 |  /*                                      | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1941 |   * Keccak block padding is: add 1 bit a | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1942 |   * then add zero bits until the end of  | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1943 |   * (the last bit in the block) - the "1 | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1944 |   * SHA3 standard appends additional two | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1945 |   *                                      | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1946 |   * SHA3-224(M) = KECCAK[448](M||01, 224 | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1947 |   * SHA3-256(M) = KECCAK[512](M||01, 256 | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1948 |   * SHA3-384(M) = KECCAK[768](M||01, 384 | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1949 |   * SHA3-512(M) = KECCAK[1024](M||01, 51 | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1950 |   * (M is the input, || is bit concatena | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1951 |   *                                      | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1952 |   * The 6 below contains 01 "SHA3" bits  | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1953 |   */                                     | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | 
1954 |  buf[ctx->bytes_queued]          ^= 6; / | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

1956 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

1958 |                                          | True                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
